                                                ==>  
22               TILE_OP_LOGU: length_o = b0[0] ? 4'd4 : 4'd3; // LOGU: uniform plane logic.
                                                -4-  
                                                ==>  
                                                ==>  
23               TILE_OP_LOGL: begin // LOGL: per-lane plane logic.
24                 known_o = available_i >= 2;
                   ==>
25                 length_o = 4'd5 + {3'b0,prefix_i[8]};
26               end
27               TILE_OP_ST, TILE_OP_ROT, TILE_OP_RED, TILE_OP_IDXR: length_o = 4'd2; // 4: ST, 8: ROT, C: RED, D: IDXR.
                 ==>
28               TILE_OP_LDX, TILE_OP_STI: length_o = 4'd3; // 5: LDX (extract), 7: STI (shifted store).
                 ==>
29               TILE_OP_ARITH, TILE_OP_CMP, TILE_OP_SHIFT: begin // 9: ADD/SUB, A: CMP, B: SHIFT.
30                 known_o = available_i >= 2;
                   ==>
31                 length_o = 4'd3 + {3'b0,((tile_opcode_e'(b0[7:4]) == TILE_OP_ARITH) ? prefix_i[9] : prefix_i[10])};
32               end

34                 // Header alone determines descriptor length: fixed two bytes, flags,
35                 // address (none/none/one/three), then the optional wide-limb byte.
36                 length_o = 4'd2 + {3'b0,b0[0]} + {3'b0,b0[1]};
37                 case (tile_amode_e'(b0[3:2]))
                   -5-

                     ==>
39                   TILE_AMODE_NEAR: length_o = length_o + 4'd1; // Near: signed word offset.
                     ==>
40                   TILE_AMODE_ABS: length_o = length_o + 4'd3; // Absolute: full address.
                     ==>
41                   default: begin end
                     ==>
42                 endcase
43               end
44               TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(b0[3:0])) // SYS: system and macro instructions.
                              -6-  
45                 TILE_SYS_ENDL, TILE_SYS_ENDC: length_o = 4'd1; // F0: ENDL, F7: ENDC.
                   ==>
46                 TILE_SYS_SETC, TILE_SYS_LD: length_o = 4'd2; // F2: SETC (commit cursor), F5: LD.
                   ==>
47                 TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_C, TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_L: length_o = 4'd4;
                   ==>
48                 TILE_SYS_MOV: length_o = 4'd3; // MOV: register-to-register move.
                   ==>
49                 TILE_SYS_LOADI: length_o = 4'd5; // LOADI: load an immediate into A.
                   ==>
50                 TILE_SYS_MUL: begin // MUL: multiply, with optional register destination.
51                   known_o = available_i >= 2;

52                   length_o = 4'd3 + {3'b0,prefix_i[9]};
53                 end
54                 default: begin end
                   ==>
55               endcase
